<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اللہ اور یوم آخرت پر ایمان رکھنے والی عورت کے لیے یہ حلال نہیں کہ وہ ایک دن اور ایک رات کا سفر کرے ۔ پھر انہوں نے اسی مفہوم کی حدیث ذکر کی جو اوپر گزری۔</p>

## Narration 1721

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1725)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يُوسُفُ بْنُ مُوسَى، عَنْ جَرِيرٍ، عَنْ سُهَيْلٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَذَكَرَ نَحْوَهُ إِلاَّ أَنَّهُ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ بَرِيدًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1725]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1725" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah reported the Messenger of Allah (SWAS) as saying :He then reported the same tradition as mentioned above but he mentioned (in this version) the word “mail post”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1725]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1725" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया जैसी रिवायत ज़िक्र की अलबत्ता इस में (दिन रात की मुसाफत के बजाये) &#x27;बुरैदान&#x27; कहा है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1725]</p>

## Narration 1722

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1726)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، وَهَنَّادٌ، أَنَّ أَبَا مُعَاوِيَةَ، وَوَكِيعًا، حَدَّثَاهُمْ عَنِ الأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَحِلُّ لاِمْرَأَةٍ تُؤْمِنُ بِاللَّهِ وَالْيَوْمِ الآخِرِ أَنْ تُسَافِرَ سَفَرًا فَوْقَ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ فَصَاعِدًا إِلاَّ وَمَعَهَا أَبُوهَا أَوْ أَخُوهَا أَوْ زَوْجُهَا أَوِ ابْنُهَا أَوْ ذُو مَحْرَمٍ مِنْهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1726]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1726" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1726</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1726 ]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Sa’id reported The Apostel of Allah (SWAS) as saying:A woman who believes in Allah and the Last Day must not make a journey of more than three days unless she is accompanied by her father or her brother, or her husband or her son or her relative who is within the prohibited degree</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1726]</p>

## Narration 1723

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1727)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي نَافِعٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تُسَافِرُ الْمَرْأَةُ ثَلاَثًا إِلاَّ وَمَعَهَا ذُو مَحْرَمٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1727]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1727" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से रिवायत करते हैं आप ने फ़रमाया: औरत तीन दिन का सफर न करे मगर इस हाल में के उसके साथ कोई मेहराम हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1727]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1727" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion practice. Attribution: Ibn Umar’s travel practice described by Nafi. Read full Safiyyah camel-seat and Mecca journey account; no Prophet attribution exists or is added.</p>

## Narration 1725

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1729)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو خَالِدٍ، - يَعْنِي سُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ حَيَّانَ الأَحْمَرَ - عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ عَطَاءٍ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ صَرُورَةَ فِي الإِسْلاَمِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1729]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1729" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सय्यदना इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: इस्लाम में &#x27;सरूरह&#x27; नहीं है. (मतलब कोई शख्स बावजूद इस्तता&#x27;आत के हज करने से ऐतराज़ कर ले).</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1729]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1729" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
